1.14.0 (2026-11-24)

🎉 Features

  • add --backgroundNewPages to keep new_page tabs in the background (#3539) (7a39d6d)
  • add abort and allow-origin network mocks (#3421) (eb4b53d)

  • add network requests from service workers (#3554) (11162da)
  • add service workers to get_console_message (#3537) (2e247a4)
  • include computed styles in get_css_styles (#3538) (cf98d8b)

  • resolve CSS custom variable values inline in get_css_styles (#3481) (8ba2ac9)
  • support transparent screenshots (#3457) (045fb8c)
  • telemetry: send startup events directly from the server process (#3511) (b14eb81)

ctx7@0.13.0 Stable2026-11-24T14:41:13Z

Minor Changes

  • 32dccfd: Agents pass the version a project uses as its own value, not as a suffix of the library ID. query-docs (MCP and pi) takes a version input and ctx7 docs a --tag option, sent to /v2/context as version; Context7 serves that version or the closest one it has. resolve-library-id no longer lists versions.

  • 11cb18d: Add --vscode-insiders to ctx7 setup and ctx7 remove. Global setup now writes to the VS Code Insiders user folder, and you can combine it with --vscode in one command. The setup picker and auto-detection do not list VS Code Insiders, so use the flag.

    ctx7 remove no longer deletes a skill from a shared skills folder (such as ~/.agents/skills) while another agent still uses it.

Patch Changes

  • 88beb24: ctx7 setup also continues with OAuth when the browser login is refused an API key by the teamspace's policy, instead of ending with "Authorization denied".
  • 82b851f: ctx7 setup continues with OAuth when the teamspace refuses the member an API key, and the CLI explains the refusal instead of a generic error.
  • 2f472ff: ctx7 setup --base-url writes a keyless MCP entry when the on-premise deployment uses OAuth sign-in, so the client signs in through the browser. --oauth now works with on-premise deployments that offer OAuth. Setup ignores a CONTEXT7_API_KEY that is not an on-premise personal key, and warns when it ignores one.
  • 7b68139: Strip terminal control characters from crowdsourced API content (library titles, descriptions, docs, code, upgrade guides and search results) before printing, preventing ANSI/OSC escape sequence injection in ctx7 output. Reported by Syed Anas Mohiuddin.

tt-a1i/archify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v3.3.1 Stable2026-11-24T14:26:08Z

Archify 3.3 in 60 seconds

https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/6a0d6637-e3af-43af-a733-4b4b53da0542

Code Analysis maps your repository as a dependency atlas and a 3D model, then marks findings on your own architecture diagram. Story turns native diagrams into animated walkthroughs you can export as SVG, a stage page or 1080p video.

What's Changed

  • release: publish stable 3.3.0 update manifest by @tt-a1i in https://github.com/tt-a1i/archify/pull/1460

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 5 releases · 5 stable
v0.9.90 Stable2026-11-24T14:04:35Z

HyperFrames v0.9.90

Released on 2026-11-24.

A project page opened straight from a link now runs apart from Studio. Sealed previews show off-canvas indicators and keep a motion path on its layer. Capture saves an inline SVG as a file only when it is a plain image.

Fixes

  • CLI: Media-use resolve reads each asset as one local self-contained container (7975ecc423, #8713).
  • Media Use: The skill's media probe opens files only as self-contained containers (7a7471e7cf, #8710).
  • CLI: Add refuses block and component scripts the project's Node tooling may run (260a8d81cc, #8703).
  • Parsers: Refuse a media path with a % and a wildcard anywhere in it (01074d93c6, #8693).
  • CLI: Capture saves an inline SVG as a file only when it is a plain image (37e4d4b75a, #8699).
  • CLI: Diff opens only media files the ledger names inside the project (fb81a9d3d3, #8695).
  • Skills: Bake-basemap asks npm where global packages live, and says where it looked (efef865806, #8698).
  • Studio: A project page opened straight from a link runs apart from Studio (7ea6e42f07, #8697).
  • CLI: Audio film queries read only sources the project may name (51f189acf3, #8694).
  • CLI: Audio --read reuses only stems this install wrote, and asks HeyGen only at best (01d77af43f, #8696).
  • Studio: Off-canvas indicators show in a sealed preview (e8ac313ed0, #8680).
  • Studio: A sealed press starts as soon as the frame answers (b9dddf3f4a, #8688).
  • Studio: A sealed picker edit answered after another pick leaves the new pick's state alone (a8f64830a4, #8686).
  • Studio Server: Refuse extensionless files where node tooling may run them (02fbd3979f, #8682).
  • Skills: Bake-basemap loads puppeteer-core only from the person's own install (fc9f4a0a46, #8681).
  • Core: Telemetry redaction stays fast on a huge token (52e565ace0, #8672).
  • Studio: A sealed motion path stays on its layer after a seek (efb797abbf, #8679).
  • Producer: A render variable bound to a media src can't add markup to the page (fb1b15b941, #8678).

Catalog

  • Registry: Show variable values as text in claude-exchange and slack-notification-ad (93f7f69de9, #8700).

v0.9.89 Stable2026-11-24T12:19:20Z

HyperFrames v0.9.89

Released on 2026-11-24.

A Studio capture shows a 4K clip from the server's frame, and deleting a clip no longer re-renders every player. File, media and backup reads touch only the one path they were given.

Fixes

  • Media Use: Carve decodes only media files inside the composition's folder (aefca2baff, #8649).
  • Player: A sealed player plays media from hosts without CORS and names a missing runtime (1c20a107a6, #8142).
  • Producer: Download links serve only the render's own file, removed once fetched (91027eafb5, #8673).
  • Studio: Class and descendant tween keyframe markers show in a sealed preview (903051c40b, #8675).
  • Skills: Remotion corpus runner uses only the CLI it is handed (5006f78508, #8677).
  • Studio: A sealed gesture recording shows every recorded value live (7b2bffef74, #8598).
  • Sdk Playground: The dev server answers only local hosts and renders composition text as text (02636023f9, #8659).
  • Core: Sub-compositions load only from inside the project (928f9eabb3, #8670).
  • Producer: A remote font stylesheet's text stays CSS when inlined (8c9e952162, #8671).
  • AWS Lambda: The handler ZIP ships ffmpeg only when it matches its pinned digest (5f0fe71cc1, #8665).
  • CLI: Check keeps copy windows for videos with #, ? or % in the name (5e1a490c80, #8669).
  • Studio: Sealed keyframe edits save the same values as a regular preview (0ee98f87d0, #8668).
  • Studio: Element picker style, data and text edits draw in a sealed preview (3ce07d4b1f, #8663).
  • Studio: Property panel corner radius and copied source line read a sealed preview (e6ba081bb9, #8662).
  • CLI: Lambda deploy and destroy use only the checkout the CLI itself is in (bfadf5b2b3, #8664).
  • Engine: Read playlists in linear time and bound the whole walk (f43c35c21d, #8653).
  • Core: Drop a text style a browser would split differently from the filter (2fee394a8f, #8658).
  • CLI: Measure refuses output paths ffmpeg would write as a pattern (b746a83e6e, #8660).
  • Skills: Refuse media paths ffmpeg would read as a pattern in skill scripts (24f474b960, #8661).
  • Studio: Paste and duplicate no longer write the copy's HTML comments (add940201e, #8657).
  • Inspector: The page server answers only its own page (03698f2511, #8654).
  • Studio Server: File routes and the received-files follower read one path (b76bb07077, #8652).
  • CLI: Check finding captures probe only the project's own videos (29a7d471ac, #8647).
  • Engine: An upstream reply the capture proxy cannot re-send fails only that request (faf4dd82a1, #8646).
  • CLI: Read every media path a command is given as that one file (de3e6153cc, #8650).
  • Skills: Raise the launcher's minimum CLI release to 0.9.80 (10b105fb26, #8651).
  • Parsers: Refuse media paths with digits between a percent sign and a wildcard (40c4f98ba4, #8648).
  • Studio Server: Studio no longer writes scripts node tooling may run (7b29b81011, #8645).
  • CLI: Timeline undo restores only project files from the project's backups (7051b2512b, #8643).
  • Studio: A sealed drag draws and saves from the open preview's rounded position (7a1828f8ee, #8587).
  • Producer: A video element naming a PNG cut short fails the render up front (5a1b992a08, #8644).
  • Publish: A release builds arm64 ffmpeg itself and publishes only its own pack (9bf40af0e5, #8641).
  • Core: A one-shot Studio capture shows a 4K clip as the server's frame instead of decoding it (c5e3ac89e4, #8626).
  • Producer: A compile's ffmpeg runs end with it, and are bounded on their own (4354b177ee, #8638).
  • Studio: A deleted clip's focus fallback no longer re-renders every player store subscriber (a383d0205b, #8633).

v0.9.88 Stable2026-11-24T05:53:38Z

HyperFrames v0.9.88

Released on 2026-11-24.

A cloud render no longer stops a chunk as stalled while its video frames are still extracting. The 4K clip on screen plays its own file while its lighter preview copy is made, instead of showing a still.

Fixes

  • Producer: A cloud chunk is not stopped as stalled while its video frames are still extracting (8cdf5b22dc, #8632).
  • Player: A film ready before load no longer times the frame's next own document from it (779bed5c0a, #8634)
  • Studio: A media reader reads only the file it was opened on (d04d8fe468, #8630).
  • CLI: Announce publish deprecation for new projects (57ffe0b07d, #8629).
  • Core: A held clip shows its own frame, not black, while its lighter copy is made (66eeb13a92, #8618)
  • Core: The 4K clip on screen plays its own file while its preview copy is made (e2fe0e6f53, #8602).
  • CLI: Layout check reads black covers as opaque and scores modern-colour gradients (c9f9a52077, #8628).
  • Studio: A failed preview's retry button takes a click again (df57e691cb, #8610).

Docs & Examples

  • The quickstart's first door opens HyperFrames Studio in the browser, the download beside it (34c7e64bb1, #8625).

v0.9.87 Stable2026-11-24T02:20:30Z

HyperFrames v0.9.87

Released on 2026-11-23.

An edit's preview shows the change again within about a second, not after a five-second wait. Looped audio stays in sync in Safari, and a render's compile now stops at a deadline.

Features

  • Studio: Apps embedding Studio can style a sealed pick as Studio's panel does (8376f32e0c, #8614).

Fixes

  • Player: A slow load fails only past its system's limit, and a late timeline still fires ready (dca98ed4c8, #8594)
  • Studio: A sealed scale resize the frame can't measure leaves no box size behind (5336bddbf9, #8620)
  • Studio: A preview frame served while its page loads keeps Studio's paused-frame decoder (0a685496ad, #8613).
  • Core: Looped audio plays in sync with the picture in Safari (32afb984bd, #8612).
  • Studio: A second sealed resize of a scaled layer no longer jumps and saves the jump (457cc56926, #8565).
  • Core: A film without a declared size reports its measured size to a sealed Studio (19f98a3ed7, #8578).
  • Studio: A waiting press says whether it waits on the frame or a save (d3c16bbe98, #8596).
  • Producer: A render's compile runs on its own thread and stops at a deadline (590617475f, #8611).
  • Studio: An unsealed recording on an empty timeline records up to the composition's end (4f863db6d7, #8606).
  • CLI: Read alpha from modern CSS colors (bb87da4536, #8624)
  • CLI: Give --json the failure text as typed for cloud commands (31be52d934, #8607).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in more lines that repeat a stored value (8948e63cb7, #8603).
  • CLI: Mention hyperframes doctor after a browser download failure (de4c73264e, #7253).
  • Media Use: Dither stages its output in a fresh folder beside the output (7717567650, #8599).
  • Studio: A ProRes clip's preview copy is no longer starved by thumbnail capture copies (bebfdd13e9, #8597).
  • Core: A sealed recording starts on a composition without GSAP (bc33d032e5, #8593).

Internal

  • Docs: The banner and top button point to HyperFrames Studio in the browser (80402d278a, #8619)
  • CLI: Count print calls whose value is not plainly the CLI's own (c03897fc11, #8608).
  • Run the studio tests in three shards so the job stops running out its time limit (18bc914d24, #8600).

Full changelog

v0.9.86 Stable2026-11-23T15:36:04Z

HyperFrames v0.9.86

Released on 2026-11-23.

A save during an import keeps its own undo step, and full-size stills count their real memory, so the cache frees them. Text on a sealed preview can now be edited in place, and sealed recordings move the layer live.

Features

  • Studio: Edit text in place on a sealed preview's canvas (8d2783561, #8183).

Fixes

  • Studio: A sealed gesture recording moves the layer live in the preview (c0753d2ad, #8592).
  • Lint: Read each linked file once and stop at one page's size limit (70d547cac, #8579).
  • Studio: A cancelled media read is not retried or logged as a failed fetch (112e52c3c, #8589).
  • Studio Server: A save asked to be recorded keeps its own undo step beside an import (540938721, #8585).
  • Studio: Keep a sealed drag's waiting outline until the frame draws its replay (2cc423b3c, #8588).
  • Studio: A sealed gesture recording keeps the layer's offset and the film's scale (40ce145f2, #8568).
  • CLI: A cloud upload leaves out a file it cannot read instead of failing (76a6bacdf, #8575).
  • Studio: Full-size composition stills count their real memory, so the cache lets them go (e238ab7fa, #8576).
  • Core: Count copied stylesheets and scene scripts toward a bundle's size limit (96abfaf0d, #8573).
  • Studio: A delete's toast names the undo key, as the ripple toast does (871333e80, #8571).

Internal

  • CLI: Describe the team descriptor in prose, not as a citation (c451acae3, #8525).
  • AWS Lambda: Build the audio sidecar test's large video in well under a second (646157c34, #8586).
  • Studio: Measure a sealed press by the element until the pointer moves (2ce48f150, #8582).

Other Changes

  • Revert "test(studio): measure a sealed press by the element until the pointer moves (#8582)" (ceaba3639, #8591).

  • Reliable delegated work: Keep completed subagent answers available across Gateway restarts and while sibling tasks are still running. (#200279) (#200649) (#200823) (#190999) (#201563) Related #188422 Authored by @steipete. Thanks @vincentkoc, @RomneyDa, @obviyus, @gouyu-hou, and @esqandil.

  • Private credentials and attachments: Preserve SecretRef-backed credentials when editing settings and avoid downloading Signal view-once attachments. (#200539) (#197319) (#201428) (#196841) Related #193149, #175333 Thanks @obviyus, @tommyjoseph, @mmaps, @anmol0b, @sallyom, @masatohoshino, and @drobison00.

Changes

  • Templates: Open a durable chat before completing verified template setup, and allow a previously deleted template agent to be added again. (#196392) (#200628) Related #198475 Thanks @Patrick-Erichsen, @DonnieFi, and @vincentkoc.

  • Managed installs: Keep releases in separate directories on OpenClaw-owned installations. (#198835) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
  • Hooks: Accept hook bodies up to 1 MiB, report rejected bodies, and handle batched Gmail pushes. (#200514) (#130002) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @garrytan and @obviyus.
  • MCP configuration: Apply saved MCP server changes from the CLI without stopping the Gateway. (#200582) Thanks @obviyus.
  • Model discovery: Refresh signed-in ChatGPT model availability independently of OpenClaw releases or npm, retain provider catalog details, and avoid advertising models omitted by the provider. (#201130) (#201493) (#202618) (#202490) (#167460) Related #201480 Thanks @RomneyDa, @obviyus, and @daniel-lxs.
  • MiniMax documents: Stop offering retired MiniMax models and enable PDF reading through the current model integration. (#203998) Thanks @RomneyDa.

Upcoming deprecations

The dates below are announced compatibility review/removal windows, not removals made by this beta. Existing removal-pending compatibility stays in place until its documented migration, published-reader, data-preservation, and approval requirements are met. Update integrations to the replacements below before their window.

  • plugin-sdk-session-agent-resolution-aliases — 2026-11-29: resolveSessionAgentIdsStrict and resolveSessionAgentIdStrict with an explicit agent, agent-scoped session key, prepared fallback, or persisted owner. See /plugins/compatibility#session-agent-resolution-aliases. Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa and @vincentkoc.
  • agent-harness-credential-prompt-string-argument — 2026-11-30: options object { controlToolsAvailable }. See /plugins/sdk-migration/removed-surfaces#credential-prompt-builder.
  • doctor-pre-july-retired-state — 2026-11-29: canonical SQLite state and byte-preserving archives for obsolete sources. See /cli/doctor/state-migrations.
  • doctor-context-budget-one-knob — 2026-11-29: models.providers.<provider>.models[].contextTokens active-input caps and per-model contextWindow metadata. See /concepts/model-providers.
  • doctor-cli-backends-plugin-registration — 2026-11-29: registerCliBackend plugin registrations and model-scoped agentRuntime.id. See /plugins/cli-backend-plugins.
  • doctor-model-compat-catalog-ownership — 2026-11-29: provider catalog compat metadata, with config compat reserved for custom routes. See /gateway/config-tools.
  • doctor-tier-eval-tranche — 2026-11-29: canonical config owners, shared SQLite state, built-in defaults, and plugin hooks. See /gateway/doctor. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • doctor-final-layout-polish — 2026-11-29: canonical final layout and built-in defaults. See /gateway/doctor.
  • doctor-phase4-product-config-retirements — 2026-11-29: built-in rescue, marketplace, banner, and retired commitments behavior. See /gateway/doctor.
  • doctor-media-models-consolidation — 2026-11-29: capability-tagged tools.media.models plus per-capability policy and defaults. See /nodes/media-understanding.
  • doctor-runtime-tuning-knobs-purge — 2026-11-29: built-in runtime and channel defaults. See /gateway/doctor.
  • doctor-phase2-channel-dm-aliases — 2026-11-29: dmPolicy and allowFrom at the same channel or account level. See /cli/doctor.
  • doctor-phase1-retired-runtime-config — 2026-11-29: canonical channel, media providerOptions, crossContext, and speakerVoice settings. See /cli/doctor. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • doctor-root-default-model — 2026-11-29: agents.defaults.model. See /gateway/doctor.
  • doctor-session-prune-reset-aliases — 2026-11-29: session.maintenance.pruneAfter; session.resetByType.direct. See /gateway/configuration-reference.
  • doctor-mcp-timeout-aliases — 2026-11-29: connectionTimeoutMs; requestTimeoutMs. See /cli/mcp.
  • doctor-cron-webhook-fallback — 2026-11-29: per-job delivery.to or delivery.completionDestination. See /automation/cron-jobs.
  • doctor-canvas-host-root — 2026-11-29: plugins.entries.canvas.config.host. See /plugins.
  • doctor-phase1-channel-noops-aliases — 2026-11-29: canonical channel settings or removal. See /channels/channel-routing.
  • doctor-memory-search-owner-consolidation — 2026-11-29: memory.search; agents.list[].memory.search. See /reference/memory-config.
  • doctor-session-typing-mode-owner — 2026-11-29: agents.defaults.typingMode or agents.list[].typingMode. See /concepts/typing-indicators. Thanks @RomneyDa and @vincentkoc.
  • doctor-mcp-server-type-alias — 2026-11-29: mcp.servers.*.transport. See /cli/mcp.
  • doctor-gateway-bind-host-aliases — 2026-11-29: gateway.bind.mode values such as lan, loopback, custom, tailnet, and auto. See /gateway/configuration.

OpenWhispr/openwhispr ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.12.11.12.1 Stable2026-11-24T11:51:57Z

OpenWhispr 1.12.1

A richer note editor, a redesigned Plans & Billing, ElevenLabs and AssemblyAI speech-to-text, and faster dictation.

🌟 Highlights

  • 📝 A richer note editor — Toggles, callouts, a table of contents, code, math, embeds and text colours, with a / menu, find & replace and [[note links]] that show where a note is linked from.
  • 🎙️ New speech-to-text options — ElevenLabs Scribe for dictation, uploads and Note Recording, AssemblyAI Dictation, and custom headers for self-hosted servers.
  • 💳 Plans & Billing redesign — Your personal plan first, a separate view per workspace, and seat purchases that preview the new total before you pay.
  • 🧩 Skills back up with Cloud — Your skills and whether each is on follow you to your other computers.
  • ⚡ Faster dictation — The mic opens and stops sooner, and the local cleanup model warms up while you speak.

✨ Also new

  • Workspace icons in Settings and the workspace switcher
  • LFM3 1.7B, VL 2B and Omni 2B local models
  • A local audit log of meeting recordings for IT, and API keys and MCP shown as managed when policy turns them off
  • Less background network traffic when the app is idle

🛠 Fixes

  • Opening Settings no longer moves Note Recording or Upload onto OpenAI.
  • Chats back up messages added after their first sync, so other devices restore whole conversations.
  • Kimi K3.1 Pro and Gemini Extra high requests no longer fail every time.
  • Cloud Whisper no longer pastes words from silence, and a microphone that drops mid-recording is picked up again.
  • Shared team notes name the person who recorded them, not whoever is viewing.
  • Laptops keep GPU Whisper after sleep. Linux: volume meters no longer keep a recording running after the call. Windows: clicks around the pill reach the window underneath. macOS 27.3: auto-paste setup works with the new app control permission picker.

Full details in the changelog.

🙏 Thanks

Thanks to @Chadpiha, @greatcoat, @boseq, @mariushm, @dan-ince-aai, @arcwel, @jaehunshin-git, @Mambokarim0731, @hsusul, @maxazure, @larsschellhas, @widechaos, @stantheman0128, @xunguangzlj-cloud and @chirag127. 🎉

Cua Driver 0.61.3

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • keep the Windows agent cursor overlay in its z-slot while idle. (#8642)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.61.2-nightly.20261124.45325875968 Prerelease2026-11-24T05:50:02Z

Cua Driver 0.61.2-nightly.20261124.45325875968 (nightly)

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

Source

  • Commit: 3151ed03b798aa88bf22cf5b8e578be6a6352f69
  • Changes: nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.59.4-nightly.20261123.45111568675…3151ed03b798

Changes

  • Fix: give the + route in the macOS permission guides. (#8572)
  • Test: load documented capability manifests through the driver loader. (#8579) reported by @teknium1.
  • Fix: draw no agent cursor for a human viewer's desktop click on Wayland. (#8581)
  • Fix: match executable grants only on the attested process image. (#8584) reported by @soarkit.
  • Feat: end the macOS hover reveal wait at the window's first repaint. (#8592) reported by @hyprcat.
  • Feat: add ui_tree_revision and refuse actions with a stale one. (#8611) reported by @orangeofcarl0-sys.
  • Fix: wait for Edge's quiet permission indicator instead of one 250 ms look. (#8620)
  • Fix: refuse macOS type_text keys when the target can't hold keyboard focus. (#8410) Thanks @AlexFalher.
  • Fix: slow the macOS permission gate's probe processes when nothing changes. (#8512) reported by @theohnx42.
  • Fix: honor x,y for background pixel clicks on macOS web content. (#8099) reported by @corywang517.
  • Fix: aim element scroll at the part inside the window. (#8142) Thanks @S3yon.
  • Fix: refuse GNOME window screenshots from helpers below API 9. (#8170) reported by @only21mil.
  • Fix: report why a Windows key press stalled, and what the doctor's form read. (#8627)

Contributors

Thanks to @AlexFalher, @corywang517, @hyprcat, @only21mil, @orangeofcarl0-sys, @S3yon, @soarkit, @teknium1, @theohnx42 for contributing to or reporting changes in this nightly.

CUA_DRIVER_RS_VERSION=nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.61.2-nightly.20261124.45325875968 /bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

cua-driver-rs-v0.61.1cua-driver-rs: v0.61.1 Prerelease2026-11-24T05:16:24Z

Cua Driver 0.61.1

Summary

This release includes 6 fixes.

Fixes

  • wait for Edge's quiet permission indicator instead of one 250 ms look. (#8620)
  • refuse macOS type_text keys when the target can't hold keyboard focus. (#8410) Thanks @AlexFalher.
  • slow the macOS permission gate's probe processes when nothing changes. (#8512) reported by @theohnx42.
  • honor x,y for background pixel clicks on macOS web content. (#8099) reported by @corywang517.
  • aim element scroll at the part inside the window. (#8142) Thanks @S3yon.
  • refuse GNOME window screenshots from helpers below API 9. (#8170) reported by @only21mil.

Contributors

Thanks to @AlexFalher, @corywang517, @only21mil, @S3yon, @theohnx42 for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.60.0

Summary

This release adds 1 feature.

Features

  • end the macOS hover reveal wait at the window's first repaint. (#8592) reported by @hyprcat.

Contributors

Thanks to @hyprcat for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

slint-ui/slint ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.19.11.19.1 Stable2026-11-24T10:13:31Z

Slint 1.19.1 is a patch release that fixes several bugs and crashes. For a complete list of changes, check out the ChangeLog.

Upgrading to Slint 1.19.1:

  • Rust: Run cargo update.
  • C++: If you're using FetchContent with CMake, update the GIT_TAG entry in CMakeLists.txt to v1.19.1 or release/1.19 for automatic tracking of 1.19.x releases.
  • Node.js: Run npm update slint-ui to bring your package.json up-to-date.
  • ESP-IDF developers: Run idf.py update-dependencies to bring your idf_component.yml up-to-date.
  • Python: Run uv lock --upgrade-package slint to update your uv lock file to the latest version.

Assets

C++ SDK

For C++ developers, this release contains the following pre-packed builds of Slint:

Wei-Shaw/sub2api ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.2.25Sub2API 0.2.25 Stable2026-11-24T03:21:08Z

AI API Gateway Platform - 将 AI 订阅配额分发和管理

API Key 账号的请求头覆写支持身份上报变量，可把本次请求的用户、密钥、分组等网关身份带给上游；账号名称与备注支持从上游分组一键或自动同步。

新增功能

  • 请求头覆写身份上报变量：API Key 账号的覆写值可使用 {{user_id}} / {{api_key_id}} / {{group_id}} / {{request_id}}，转发时替换为本次请求的网关身份；编辑器新增变量面板、发送示例预览与行内错误提示
  • 错误详情新增「身份上报请求头」区块，展示该次请求实际上报给上游的身份头
  • 上游分组名称与备注同步：创建/编辑账号及账号列表可一键把上游分组名称、描述同步为账号名称与备注
  • 自动同步上游分组名称与备注：每个 API Key 账号可单独开启（默认关闭），随上游倍率探测一并更新
  • /v1/sub2api/billing 接口支持 include=group，返回 Key 所绑定分组的名称与描述

优化改进

  • OpenAI 透传模式支持管理员配置的错误透传规则（透传上游错误体、自定义文案、跳过监控）
  • OpenAI 透传模式下的 400 invalid_encrypted_content 与上下文超长错误保留结构化的 type / code / param，客户端可据此正确处理

Bug 修复

  • 修复 OpenAI 透传错误脱敏在遇到特殊字符时可能崩溃或泄露上游主机名的问题
  • 修复 compact 流式响应中上游过载类错误未改写为可重试错误，导致 Codex 直接终止会话的问题
  • 修复上游分组名称或描述含非法字符时，倍率与探测结果整体写入失败并被反复重试的问题

📥 Installation

Docker:

# Docker Hub
docker pull weishaw/sub2api:0.2.25

# GitHub Container Registry
docker pull ghcr.io/wei-shaw/sub2api:0.2.25

One-line install (Linux):

curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Wei-Shaw/sub2api/main/deploy/install.sh | sudo bash

Manual download: Download the appropriate archive for your platform from the assets below.

📚 Documentation

  • GitHub Repository
  • Installation Guide

  • 🔔 系统通知 (Notifications): Gemini 回复完成后可发送 macOS 通知，并直达对应对话。 macOS can notify you when a Gemini response finishes and open the matching conversation.
  • 🔄 自动更新 (Automatic updates): 完成这次迁移后，后续版本可通过 Sparkle 自动更新。 After this one-time migration, future versions can update automatically through Sparkle.

vercel-labs/agent-browser ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.44.0 Stable2026-11-23T22:44:34Z

New Features

  • Added a Ladybird provider: -p ladybird drives Ladybird over WebDriver, headless by default or with --headed. Safari and Ladybird share one local driver launcher, and the WebDriver client now honors Content-Length, so it no longer hangs on drivers that keep the connection open. Snapshots and refs stay Chrome-only, as with Safari (#2721)
  • Added background tab controls: tab keep live [tab] keeps a background tab running at full speed instead of throttled, tab keep throttled returns it to the default, and tab keep frozen <tab> pauses it until the agent switches back. The daemon unfreezes a tab before any command runs on it and when the session closes (#2624, #2627)
  • Added right-click, middle-click, and modifier keys to click and dblclick with --button and --modifiers. A double click now sends two full clicks (#2594)
  • WebMCP announcements now tell agents what the page's tools are and when to use them, list each tool as [origin] name [frame]: description with full descriptions for small catalogs, mark tools from other origins as cross-origin, and include a ready-to-run webmcp invoke command for the page's own tools. webmcp invoke also accepts the JSON input right after the tool name (#2606, #2591, #2720)
  • Added feature flag overrides to the vercel skill, renamed from protected-vercel-deployments. Agents set the Flags SDK override cookie so only their own browser sees a variant, instead of changing the flag for everyone on the environment (#2441)

Behavior Changes

  • Unknown flags are rejected across command parsers instead of being read as selectors or file paths, or ignored. Arguments after -- stay literal in the CLI and MCP (#2665)
  • Refs from a replaced document fail with a "no longer loaded" error after a full page load or an iframe navigation, instead of acting on the wrong element. Take a new snapshot after each load (#2685)
  • fill, type, check, uncheck, and select check the target first and refuse with a stable code (not_editable, read_only, disabled, not_checkable, option_not_found, ambiguous, not_multiple, malformed_value) without changing the field. fill sets date, time, color, and range inputs directly, select --by value or --by label resolves an ambiguous option, and a value the page changes afterward is reported as a warning (#2687)
  • snapshot -s <sel> covers every match, not only the first. The cursor-interactive scan now refs <summary>, native controls with non-interactive roles, and clickables inside open shadow roots, without writing attributes to the page (#2688)
  • upload, network har stop, diff snapshot, and diff url check their preconditions. upload resolves relative paths against the CLI's working directory and fails on a missing file or a target that is not a file input, har stop fails when nothing is recording, diff snapshot without --baseline compares against the tab's last snapshot, and diff url prints its result and honors --screenshot and --full (#2689)
  • --restore, --restore-save, and --restore-check-* travel only through the settings patch, like the other session settings. A save policy without a key applies to the running key, a key change on a command that never launches closes the browser without relaunching it, and get cdp-url and session info leave the restore settings alone. A launch that fails after a key change closes the new browser without saving (#2667)

Security

  • Stream server /api routes now require a same-origin request and a loopback Host, and rejected requests carry no CORS headers (#2678)
  • Stream WebSocket upgrades require the Origin to match the Host, so a page on another localhost port can no longer read frames or inject input (#2682)
  • An allowlist that lists no domain is an error from the flag, environment, config, or MCP, instead of turning containment off. An empty AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS now fails for commands that run a browser; unset it to run without containment. chat no longer lets the model answer confirm or deny (#2683)
  • MCP tool arguments can no longer be read as global CLI flags, and flag-shaped values in fields that are not passed literally are refused (#2681)
  • Saved flow replay sends only each command's interaction fields and runs under the current session's settings, plugins, and policy (#2607)
  • Daemon warnings travel out of band, so page-controlled JSON can no longer forge postActionWarning or sessionSetupWarning (#2693)
  • Session lock files are created owner-only on Unix (#2676)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed clicks on elements at the viewport edge or that reflow missing while reporting success. Clicks, hover, and tap now scroll the point into view, wait for layout to settle, and press the settled point (#2587)
  • Fixed ref clicks inside nested and cross-origin frames landing on the parent page. The press is verified to reach the ref, an element it activates, or a frame it embeds (#2611)
  • Fixed refs lost after a failed snapshot. Snapshot, snapshot diff, and annotated screenshots replace refs only on success (#2666)
  • Fixed session setup lost on relaunch. Viewport, user agent, media, timezone, locale, geolocation, headers, offline mode, and init scripts carry across a relaunch and apply to new tabs and windows, and set device mobile presets enable touch emulation (#2686)
  • Fixed select on custom ARIA comboboxes and listboxes such as Radix and Headless UI, which reported success without changing anything (#2592)
  • Fixed env and config connection targets replacing a browser attached with connect. AGENT_BROWSER_CDP, AGENT_BROWSER_PROVIDER, AGENT_BROWSER_AUTO_CONNECT, and their config keys now apply only to a session with no target, and CDP headers are bound to their endpoint (#2642)
  • Fixed two concurrent first commands spawning two daemons for one session (#2648)
  • Fixed direct-page providers dropping page events, which left logs empty and recordings unable to finish (#2445)
  • Fixed WebMCP on Chrome 157 and later, and cross-origin iframes timing out under --allowed-domains on Chrome 157 (#2700)
  • Fixed temporary Chrome profiles left behind by killed daemons. Each launch removes profiles older than two hours whose Chrome is gone (#2586)
  • Fixed installs under Volta leaving a dangling global link, and failed fallback downloads leaving a 0-byte binary (#2675)
  • Fixed pin-tab taking two paths that could disagree; it now travels only through the settings patch (#2643)

Documentation

  • Launched the agent-browser.dev homepage with a Browser/Desktop switcher. The docs introduction moved to /introduction (#2411, #2625, #2626, #2638, #2658, #2659, #2717, #2718, #2719)

FlashML-org/FreeToken ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.2.3 Stable2026-11-23T22:25:53Z

Highlights

  • Gemma-4 MTP. --speculative-algorithm mtp now drafts for Gemma-4 (dense, E2B / E4B and MoE) with an mtp-*.gguf assistant draft passed through --speculative-draft-model-path (#895, #903).
  • KV cache quantization. --kv-cache-dtype fp8_per_token_head or int8_per_token_head stores K and V in 8 bits per element, about half the KV memory (#894).
  • DeepSeek-V4.1 GGUF. llama.cpp's deepseek41 checkpoints serve text (#890).

KV Cache Quantization

--kv-cache-dtype fp8_per_token_head (e4m3) and int8_per_token_head store K and V in 8 bits per element plus one fp32 scale per token and head, about half the KV memory. The triton, qsa_sparse, m3_sparse and dsa attention backends read them. They need --dtype bfloat16 and TP 1. The default, auto, keeps the compute dtype. DeepSeek-V4 now stores its window and compressed KV as fp8_ds_mla rows, 584 B instead of 1024 B. See the flag.

  • [Feature] Add fp8_per_token_head and int8_per_token_head KV cache formats (#894)
  • [Feature] Add fp8_ds_mla and nvfp4_ds_mla KV formats for DeepSeek-V4 / V4.1 (#920)

Speculative Decoding

  • [Feature] --speculative-algorithm mtp on Gemma-4 (dense, E2B / E4B and MoE), with an mtp-*.gguf assistant draft passed through --speculative-draft-model-path (#895, #911)
  • [Feature] Add --speculative-token-map for a smaller MTP draft head (#892)
  • [Feature] Load DFlash 2 GGUF drafts for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (#914)
  • [Perf] Capture the Gemma-4 assistant draft in CUDA graphs (#903)
  • Not supported yet: TP above 1, targets with sliding-window KV other than Gemma-4, and targets with DSV4 window-tier KV.

GGUF Support

  • [Feature] Serve DeepSeek-V4.1 GGUF checkpoints on deepseek_v41 (#890)
  • [Perf] Decode DeepSeek-V4.1's wo_a from its ggml blocks (#915, #949)

Model Support

  • [Feature] Serve the Gemma-4 E2B and E4B releases, text only, from HF safetensors or GGUF (#59)
  • [Perf] Read the source layer's KV slab on Gemma-4 KV-shared layers (#898)

Quantization

  • [Feature] Compress bf16 weights to DF11 at load with --online-quant df11 (#891)
  • [Fix] Accept the modelopt_mixed quant method (#886)

Engine

  • [Perf] Split the CPU executor's work by expert when tokens share one (#916)
  • [Fix] Pad fp8 calibration scale banks like the loader in ft bench bw (#902)

Kernels

  • [Perf] Run up to 8 query rows per request in the Triton decode kernel (#900, #906)

Server and CLI

  • [Feature] Report timings on every streamed chunk with timings_per_token (#887)
  • [Fix] Send an empty signature when a streamed thinking block starts (#889)
  • [Fix] Render the developer role as system for templates without it (#904)

AMD ROCm (WIP, experimental)

  • [Perf] Add packed fp8 KV reads on gfx1201 (#897)

Breaking Changes & Deprecations

  • GLM-4.7 serves its attention and input embedding in bf16, as the checkpoint stores them. Earlier builds always compressed them to DF11; pass --online-quant df11 to keep that (about 8 GiB less VRAM). An FTW of GLM-4.7 converted by an earlier build stores those weights as DF11 and no longer loads; reconvert it with ft checkpoint (#891).

Contributors

@jason-fxz, @ischencheng, @void-mckenzie, @coco-alen, @mkornreich, @LZ-QWQ, @zihaomu, @taking-lying-flat

First contributions this release:

  • @mkornreich in #59
  • @coco-alen in #889

redhat-et/ripwire ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.7.0 Stable2026-11-23T21:27:46Z

[0.7.0] — 2026-11-23

ripwire 0.7.0 is practical for large repositories on small and normal-sized machines. On an Apple M5 Pro (48 GB) it indexes llvm-project cold in 28 s at a 5.1 GB peak, where two of five peer tools stopped at our one-hour limit and one failed; the same llvm-project runs (map cold and warm, --for, --deps, --quality-delta) complete on a 16 GB Apple M2 laptop. 0.6.9 and earlier could exhaust memory on llvm-project (one process reached 149.7 GB).

117 merged pull requests since 0.6.10 (#456 to #718), plus #710; 0.6.11 was skipped, so this release carries everything since 0.6.10. What a user sees (each entry below states its own measurement and its floors):

Measured against five other code-context tools on one machine (the README's "How ripwire compares"): on 213 held-out tasks, one call each, ripwire's answer was complete on 126, against 68 for the next tool; it indexed llvm-project in 28 s at a 5.1 GB peak, where two of the five stopped at our one-hour limit and one failed.

Full tables (speed, memory, answers; method and setup notes): README — How ripwire compares

  • Answers: more of what a task needs, and fewer confident wrong ones.
    • --for prints the manifest lines that define a package, dependency, binary, build target, feature or entry point the task names (package.json, Cargo.toml, pyproject.toml, setup.cfg, go.mod, CMakeLists.txt); on the 321 in-sample calls 8 changed, +2 required items, 0 lost, +0.188 % bytes (#565).
    • --for lists who calls the function that reads a literal, names the call that makes a head name's value and the declaration that holds a dispatch candidate, names the function that stores a head row into a slot, says when a hop row's function is used as a value, and points INTO the one holder on an add-a-new-X task. A sentence gets the callers, path and connect blocks its bare names get; a task that spells an import gets the chain from the spelling to the definition; a short "what does X call" question keeps its source anchor.
    • --path, --connect and --callers bind a refused spelling's one source definition; --path says when its verdict is one of many unwalked definition pairs and Owner.method narrows instead of refusing; --connect never names a test function as the joint of two source symbols.
    • A language you state orders name answers by language: Shape@rs, a file lane or the LSP client's declared language puts that language's definition first, name@lang works for every symbol verb (22 of 22 selectors answered the stated language on cpython, envoy and vscode; all were "symbol not found" before), and reflang= says when the language decided (#440, thanks @mpapis).
    • An interface method, a C++ pure virtual or a Go interface method no longer answers a confident zero for --impact, --callers, --safe-delete and --path; a zero or "not found" that means "not indexed" says so; --exemplar hedges an off-task pick; --help-task abstains with a next step; a <cycle> that lists only 12 of its files says so (members_capped="1", #479, thanks @jflowers); --lego says "unread for this language"; lexical scans read every file and a reached limit is printed to stderr; a --for budget only trims the answer.
    • Answers are smaller where they repeated themselves: --for legends define only what the answer carries, repeated <d> text and tail names print once, deep same-file siblings and one-endpoint rows become tail pointers, and a tail row names up to two of its file's symbols that share the task's words (tsym=). --legend-have= takes legend tiers, so the handshake pays for itself in about ten calls instead of about 130.
  • Speed and memory.
    • Each indexed reference takes 120 B less (264 to 144 B per row): peak footprint on large repositories 10 to 24 % lower (macOS, median of three; mem-ref-shrink). On the M5 table's machine (Apple M5 Pro, 48 GB, 2026-11-22) ripwire peaks at 0.7 GB indexing envoy and 1.1 GB indexing vscode.
    • A warm run on an unchanged tree reads back the crawl, the model and the resolved graph instead of rebuilding them; the cache file does not change and nothing is re-indexed (warm-snapshot). A cold JavaScript/TypeScript index retires 24 % fewer instructions on vscode (js-capture-cold); cold indexing climbs the syntax tree from a cached path instead of the root; the exact clone pass keys each function body at parse time and no longer reads every file on a warm run (cockroach was about 9,500 files per warm --for).
    • Large repos: llvm-project cold-indexes in 28 s at a 3.7 GB peak, and 5.1 GB peaks including the first question; ripwire indexes 80,576 of the 180,650 files git tracks (the M5 table's machine, above). The same runs (map cold and warm, --for, --deps, --quality-delta) complete on a 16 GB Apple M2 laptop. Three changes carry it: the name-walk fix (0.6.9 and earlier could exhaust memory on llvm-project), the allocator-level memory ceiling (a runaway step now exits 5 instead of taking the host down), and the graph-store work (120 B less per indexed reference, a warm run that reads back the resolved graph).
    • An allocator-level memory ceiling is on by default at the memory guard's own limit, and a map of llvm-project no longer grows without bound (a name-keyed class walk had no visited set: one process reached 149.7 GB; the cold map now peaks at 3.7 GB on the M5, Release build with profile-guided optimization, output byte-identical).
    • The default map's inventory tier is bounded (vscode's <ls> was 82.4 % of the document); the cross-branch verbs answer in repositories with more than 512 local branches; gitFileAuthors (behind --owners and --pr-context) uses a flat table and zero-allocation commit parsing (#473, thanks @lennix1337; byte-identical, CPU and memory unchanged within noise).
    • The prompt-routing hooks no longer time out on every prompt in a cold directory: --help-task=TASK --deadline-ms=N answers within the deadline and finishes the index in the background (#594, thanks @WingedGuardian).
  • Quality-delta (--quality-delta).
    • The default legend defines fix= on a row with no why= (a complexity row with a repeated-decision fix=); other answers keep their bytes (#710).
    • tangle is the thirteenth kind: a long function whose nesting goes deep, whose deep regions take a growing share, or whose steering locals multiply, with a best-since-tag ratchet and an <improved> section; named-step orchestrators score 0.
    • New: error-masking facet="failure-default", a dropped I/O or child status (defect="unchecked-status"), a classifier that leaves a value without an outcome (defect="unhandled-arm"), adjacent same-typed parameters (similar=, report-only), and every pull request runs --quality-delta over its whole range (qd-range CI job, scripts/qd_range.sh).
    • Fewer false rows: renaming or moving a function no longer reads as new code (75 placed runs over nine corpora: 18 false-alarm rows, 4 gating, to 0), a file rename git recorded keeps every function's identity, a short clone row over different data and a pair that crossed the bar only because one member shrank stop gating, a new function at the ceiling (4x the bar) now gates.
    • Warm runs are cheaper: the near-clone comparison runs 64 cells per machine word (46 % envoy, 82 % cockroach, 9 % vscode fewer instructions) and error-masking rows live in the cache record (43 / 27 / 69 % fewer).
  • Languages.
    • C#, C++ and Java base and import clauses are read through the shared tags-capture paths (#420 LaimaWu, #538 and #606 SeddonShen); the tags pass evaluates string predicates and Elixir's keyword tables moved to tags.scm (#426 conka8; #any-of? and #not-any-of? now filter in --match too).
    • Rust: a call written inside a macro's arguments is a call of the enclosing function (axum 4,885 to 5,158 call edges, nushell 52,405 to 58,051, zed 288,843 to 306,195); a macro invoked by path or use ... as alias reaches its in-tree macro_rules!; builder chains resolve through -> Self; <T as Trait>::m() names T's impl; an unresolved pub use no longer hides an in-tree re-export (#610, thanks @soarkit); #[test] functions and Drop::drop are no longer dead code (#493, #494, #499, #500, thanks @zom-2018).
    • C and C++: calls through smart-pointer locals and aliases reach the interface tier, a call no longer binds a same-named base class in another namespace, a macro invocation or trailing annotation macro is no longer stored as a definition. Go: method expressions are references. Python: from pkg import mod is a file edge to pkg/mod.py (#478, #533, thanks @jflowers) and an import inside a function gives that function its module calls. TypeScript: a call through an in-root paths alias (@/x) binds through its import (#711, reported by SStonkSS1). Clojure: a call whose list head carries metadata is extracted (#715 himanchalkaushale, #548 first arm) and the inert-region walk is shared (#456, LaimaWu).
    • New: Protocol Buffers (.proto) definitions are indexed and a generated name links to its schema by name; --flags reports Bash environment-variable reads, child shells, local scope, redirections and quoting included, with unreadable= for a file it cannot read (#641 and #685, jflowers); --recall ranks document sections and HTML pages have a section tree.
  • MCP and wrap. MCP find_referencing_symbols binds a narrowed callers selector like --callers; the prompt hooks answer within --deadline-ms (above); a build off a release tag says so in its version (0.6.10-dev.41+g...).
  • Fixes and hardening. --regex and every user-pattern entry point (--arch rules, --graph-query file(), #match?) refuse the multi-quantifier family that made libc++ abandon matches and libstdc++ never return (#484, thanks @pt-act; 88-assertion gate, red on main with 15 FAIL); two release-build undefined-behavior sites on adversarial input are gone; the mem= legend names every key of the order it prints, owner's file, path, line and name (#595, #682, thanks @soarkit); two benchmark harnesses refuse a malformed bank (#540 NopeCharon, reported by soarkit in #492); ordering ties break on content keys, not node id, so the same answer prints on every machine.
  • The answer-first stack (#710; its 88 fragments name no community contributor).
    • Highlights. On llvm-project the name-keyed and C++ base-class walks enter each name once, so a map that once grew until the OS killed it now takes 28 s (answers unchanged byte for byte). Bare --lint, --expand and the flagless map are held to a 24,000-character ceiling with one pasteable next= call (largest map 52,858 to 23,629 characters, largest --lint 83,162 to 19,589, none over 30,000 on the 11 development repositories). A --for answer names the ranges to read next in one <act read="path:l-e,…"/> element, for parallel ranged Reads (RIPWIRE_NO_ACTLINE=1 turns it off). skills/install.sh installs a ripwire-locator Claude Code subagent that returns at most 12 lines of edit ranges; no token saving has been measured yet.
    • Answers.
      • A --for question that names its symbol in backticks and asks who calls it, where it is used or what breaks is answered by --callers, --uses or --impact, and the answer says so first (<route from="for" verb= complete="not-proven" next=>); warn="1" why= marks a verb likely to miss a macro caller, a callback slot, twin definitions or a cut list, and then the first --for page rides along. --no-verb-route (MCP no_verb_route) opts out. Call paths and chains ("the call chain from A to B"), questions that quote a literal message and "what does X call" are never routed. A windowed routed answer continues itself (--for=Q --offset=K), and a routed answer is held to the 12,000 B page (one warned question went from 30,369 to 20,727 B on 89 dev questions).
      • A who-calls --for puts the callers block first (bytes until the whole answer appeared: median 6,147 to 2,618 on 43 held-out questions) and its page 1 ends early when that block proves itself complete (early="proven", 2 of 52 held-out answers, each still complete).
      • Page 1 of a --for question that names a symbol carries that symbol's body (<bodies of="key">, up to 4,000 B each, 5 bodies at most; a body is capped at 120 lines with cap_lines=); the key group keeps its own copy whatever else the page serves. --bodies=pointer names the bodies instead of carrying them (default unchanged, page 1 a median 715 B smaller on 60 of 89 pages; whether it lowers a session's tokens is untested).
      • --pack-task is one bundle again: the --for head, the file tail and ONE key body, the tail takes its share as a floor (bundles that name every file the question needs: 329 to 420 of 596 tasks on four sets), and est_tokens= and budget_tokens= use one conversion.
      • Ranking and paging: a symbol's file basename is a ranking field (every-file-and-function complete 66 to 67 on the 89 dev questions, body rung 24 to 27, no cell down); the sigs rows past rank 10 are short on page 1 (<sigx> carries their text), carry e= beside l= (usable line ranges for 80 to 96 of 123 gold functions), and a tail past row 24 lists the files in a top-5 row's directory first; an explicit priority keeps a body from displacing a research section, and a body now reaches page 1 behind a tail that fits (edit-function bodies on page 1: 9 to 20 of 89 cells with the guarded promotion, 18 to 21 with the lower body guarantee). A paged page 1 no longer defines what only a later page carries. A budgeted answer no longer stops one row short, and docs_after_code= counts the rows still shown.
      • --for serves its <sigs> and <tail> rows as text lines (--rows=elements / MCP rows opt out; decoded, byte for byte the element form; page 1 a median 7.6 % smaller; on one model the line form cost about twice the output tokens when asked to list the rows, and the effect in a real session is untested).
      • --callers, --uses and --impact name the definitions and declarations they measured (<def> rows, an untied declaration listed bodyless="1"): complete 12 to 27 of 48 for --callers and 15 to 32 for --uses on held-out gold, at +9.33 % and +15.49 % bytes, which failed the pre-registered +2 % bar and landed on completeness. --impact puts production callers and evidence-bearing importers in its first window. A found="0" answer names the plain-text occurrences (text_hits=, next="--grep=NAME"); --grep names the files whose hits its tier held back (held_files=) and no longer holds hits back in a file whose parse degraded; --affected, --test-gate and --situ answer for the files that resolve and name each unusable one (unused, not_indexed or not_found).
      • Graph edges: --callers and --uses see through one C function-like macro (via="macro:NAME", at most 16 files per macro); a C function stored through a registrar's parameter into a struct field names who may call it (tmux client_signal); a C static in a header binds from the files that include it; a call inside a Rust macro_rules! transcriber is a call of that macro (axum 4,885 to 5,076 edges) and a matcher word is not a call.
      • A served body starts where its declaration starts (export, decorators, template<...>, #[...]): 45 of 103 language-by-form rows lost their first line or keyword before; --insert-before-symbol on a decorated definition lands above the decorators.
    • Speed and memory. The memory limit is also read at operator new and a runaway allocation exits 5 with one stderr line (RIPWIRE_MEM_BUDGET_MB sets that line alone); the header-static question builds its include graph from C-family #includes alone (5.9 % fewer instructions on a warm cpython --for).
    • Tokens and bytes. --legend-seen=FILE leaves out the legend definitions you already hold (a hook builds the file from the answers a session received); a skills/install.sh --hook install now registers the hook by default and it REWRITES ripwire commands (shown before it writes; --no-hook-legend-seen or RIPWIRE_LEGEND_SEEN=0 turns it off); three more --for notes are held entries; the <def> and <f> readings are shorter; --affected groups fold their paths (pf=, lossless); MCP tools/list is shorter with the tool contract byte-identical; SKILL.md bodies are short with the rest in reference files (32 of 33 blind-rated tasks scored with both). --legend-have= on a non-XML answer says not-applicable.
    • Quality-delta. Two quality-delta findings the batch carried (a four-parameter legend function, a complexity-16 function) are fixed rather than acked; no output moves. A comment is no longer counted as a language predicate by the identity ratchet, which discloses its count as a floor.
    • MCP and wrap. MCP for serves rows as lines too (rows argument) and a routed MCP answer is held to the page and continued through offset=/limit=; ripwire wrap claude prints a three-moment block to paste into every subagent brief, skills/install.sh --hook also registers a SubagentStart note for Explore and Plan subagents, an opt-in primer (--hook-subagent-primer) is available, and the edit-routing line no longer says a Read always precedes an Edit (measured on stored sessions: 4 refusals in 283 Edits, all outside the working directory). Delivery into the subagent has not been run live.
    • Fixes. The MSVC build reads the two --for route cue tables again; the ASan build answers two-endpoint --for tasks (an unsigned hop count wrapped); the HEAD-binary build and the bench harnesses are bounded and record a timeout or a kill as its own state; the showcase capture regenerates on a merge-heavy tip; undefined legend attributes are defined where they appear; a gate for bounding the carried page landed and its mechanism was reverted because it shrank answers silently.
  • Contributors: Bash environment reads, from pkg import mod and the capped cycle list (@jflowers: #641, #685, #533, #541); C++ and Java base captures (@SeddonShen: #538, #606); the regex guard (@pt-act: #484, #691); C# imports and Clojure's shared inert-region walk (@LaimaWu: #420, #456); tags-pass string predicates (@conka8: #426); bench refusals (@NopeCharon: #540); the flat gitFileAuthors table (@lennix1337: #473); Rust dead-code exemptions (@zom-2018: #499, #500); language-first name answers (@mpapis: #440); Clojure metadata heads (@himanchalkaushale: #715); reports that led to fixes (@soarkit: #492, #595, #610, #611; @WingedGuardian: #594; @SStonkSS1: #711; @pt-act: #692).
  • Thanks: @jflowers, @SeddonShen, @pt-act, @LaimaWu, @conka8, @NopeCharon, @lennix1337, @zom-2018, @mpapis, @himanchalkaushale, @soarkit, @WingedGuardian and @SStonkSS1.

Every entry of this release, with its measurement and its floors, is in CHANGELOG.md.

coreyhaines31/marketingskills ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v2.14.28v2.14.28 — Three methods added to existing skills (#1048). Stable2026-11-23T21:01:10Z

Three methods added to existing skills (#1048).

  • prospecting (1.7.0 → 1.8.0): podcast appearances as a lead source. Episodes are indexed with speaker-labelled transcripts, so a search finds a named guest describing the problem you solve, in their own words. references/signal-plays.md covers three uses (find people describing the problem, research an account's leader, find the shows your buyers appear on) and five rules: check the air date, confirm who said it on the episode page, apply ICP fit, name the episode openly because they published it, and keep the reference to one line. A 90-day freshness window and a tools row are added. Eval 15 added.
  • social (2.6.0 → 2.6.1), marketing-loops (1.9.0 → 1.9.1): outlier posts are scored against the account's own median, split by platform and format, as a multiple of the baseline (1.5–2× mild, 2–5× strong, 5× or more huge), from at least 20 posts, with low confidence under 10. This replaces "for example 100K+ views" in the listening reference and the niche-outlier loop. Method adapted from ScrapeCreators' outlier-post-finder skill (MIT).
  • customer-research (2.0.8 → 2.0.9): a table for sorting a comment thread into questions, objections, complaints, confusion, requests, buying intent, debate, and praise before clustering, with two cautions: the top comments are not the representative ones, and commenters are an audience, not a list.

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 53 skills. Claude web target: 53 skills with reviewed short descriptions. Perplexity Computer target: 53 skills.

Choose <name>-claude-web.zip for the documented single-folder web upload format. Choose <name>-perplexity.zip for Perplexity Computer (SKILL.md at the archive root). Source ZIPs retain original metadata; they are not substitutes for omitted web ZIPs. Live client upload, account limits, and invocation behavior have not been verified.

Web omissions (also recorded in claude-web-manifest.json):

  • None.

v2.14.27v2.14.27 — New tool access for app store, SEO audit, comment, and podcast data (#1048). Stable2026-11-23T20:58:21Z

New tool access for app store, SEO audit, comment, and podcast data (#1048).

  • aso (2.0.6 → 2.1.0): a new section, With App Store Connect Access. For the user's own Apple app, an agent with the open-source App Store Connect CLI reads reviews by rating and country, the rating breakdown, Apple's review summaries, week-over-week numbers, and the exact listing text, always in read-only mode, in place of asking for an export. Review responses and metadata changes are drafted and sent only on approval. New guide: tools/integrations/app-store-connect-cli.md, plus an App Store category in the registry.
  • seo-audit (2.2.9 → 2.2.10): SEOmator joins Tools Referenced, an open-source audit CLI and local MCP server an agent or CI job can run free. Checks it couldn't run stay "not measured" in the report. New guide: tools/integrations/seomator.md. Its repository ships a skill also named seo-audit; the guide covers the CLI and MCP server only.
  • ScrapeCreators guide: comment and transcript endpoints, for mining what an audience asks and objects to. The wording is kept; lists of commenters are not.
  • Firecrawl guide: podcast transcripts through Alexandria (speaker-labelled, quotes with timestamps), with the documented request, credit costs, and the third-party terms step that needs the user.

  • prospecting (1.6.3 → 1.7.0): subprocessor and DPA pages as a prospecting source (#1046). Companies publish the vendors that process their customers' data, so the page is a free, first-party record of part of the stack. references/signal-plays.md gains a section on three uses: finding accounts that name a given vendor ("subprocessors" "<vendor>"), qualifying an account before writing, and catching a vendor being added or dropped. It says where the pages live, and gives rules for the weak points: the list covers only vendors that touch customer data, absence proves nothing, names may be legal entities, and the lookup is never the opening line. The tools table, the tech-stack signal row, and references/data-sources.md list it. Eval 14 added.

mengxi-ream/read-frog ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.61.2 Stable2026-11-23T17:49:07Z

Patch Changes

  • #2561 7f51190 Thanks @ananaBMaster! - style(providers): show the colored Claude logo for the Anthropic provider and Claude models

  • #2562 b671615 Thanks @ananaBMaster! - fix(context-menu): make the right-click actions on a selection work in the readfrog.app ebook reader

  • #2572 0fe2a30 Thanks @ananaBMaster! - fix(providers): let guests pick the Built-in AI models signing in unlocks

    Logged-out users can now choose Built-in AI models in the provider menus, the popup and the options page instead of seeing them grayed out. Only models that need a plan the account would still lack after signing in, such as the Ultra models, stay locked, the same as for a free account. The menus' "LLM Models" group is now called "Third-party Models".

  • #2536 38ed1d0 Thanks @taiiiyang! - fix(subtitles): keep the subtitles a reader picks before an embedded video first plays. With subtitles set to start automatically, the first play of an X or embedded YouTube video replaced AI subtitles asked for beforehand with the video's own captions and turned back on subtitles the reader had switched off

  • #2565 8f83fb6 Thanks @ananaBMaster! - feat(providers): add GPT-6.2, Fable 5.2 and Gemini 4 models and drop retired ones

    Add GPT-6.2 Sol and Astra, Claude Fable 5.2, Gemini 4 Carbon, Hydrogen and Neon, Nova 2 Lite and Fable 5.2 on Bedrock, GLM 5.3 on Mistral, and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash on OpenRouter, DeepInfra, Hugging Face, AtlasCloud and Alibaba, with thinking turned off like V4 Flash. Drop the models OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, Moonshot, Groq, Cerebras and Mistral have shut down, and move providers that used them to the recommended replacement. Moonshot now defaults to Kimi K3, because Kimi K2.6 is closed to new Kimi accounts. DeepSeek now defaults to deepseek-flash, which serves V4.1 Flash, and providers on the retired deepseek-v4-flash move to it.

  • #2564 c8f922c Thanks @ananaBMaster! - fix(selection): keep page content added after Read Frog's UI in Cmd/Ctrl+A on Chrome

  • #2573 b3f0ad0 Thanks @ananaBMaster! - feat(subtitles): show translations right away at the start of a video and after a seek

    AI subtitles now send every line of the next 30 seconds at once, instead of one batch at a time. A line about to show before its AI translation arrives gets a Microsoft machine translation in the meantime. A line already on screen keeps that translation until it ends, and the transcript shows the AI translation at once. A batch that fails is now sent again 10 seconds later, or as soon as another batch comes back, instead of leaving its lines untranslated. While the line on screen has failed and no batch has come back since, the error shows in place of the subtitles, and it goes away by itself once a batch comes back. When the error is one every later batch would get too, such as a wrong key (401), an empty balance (402, or an OpenAI account with no balance), no permission (403), a wrong model (404), or Built-in AI refusing, translation stops at the first failure: the machine translations are removed, and the error stays on screen in place of the subtitles until you come back to the tab or change the subtitles provider. A subtitle error from a provider with your own key now starts with that provider's name, such as "OpenAI: You exceeded your current quota…", so it does not read as Read Frog's own. For a wrong key or no permission (401, 403), a rate limit (429) or a server error (500, 502, 503), the error now says so in Read Frog's words, as it was meant to; other errors, and an empty balance, keep the provider's own message. A long error is now cut short at the player's edge, with the whole text on hover. An OpenAI account with no balance is no longer retried as a rate limit for several minutes. Looking up a word in a subtitle now uses only the original line as context.

  • #2575 b5a29b9 Thanks @ananaBMaster! - fix(i18n): update outdated Built-in AI and provider copy across locales

    Updates Built-in AI standard and Ultra model descriptions, reserves "Built-in" wording for Read Frog's hosted models instead of preset third-party providers, unifies Built-in model labels across all 10 locales, and updates outdated OpenAI and API key warning copy.

  • #2559 2554145 Thanks @taiiiyang! - feat(subtitles): request AI subtitles on X videos whose player shows no media id, such as uploads with a custom cover and live broadcast replays in a post. The server transcribes the post's only video. A replay's own captions arrive only a few seconds ahead of playback, so it now gets AI subtitles instead of a translation of its first seconds; GIFs and broadcasts still live get none

  • #2539 85008c1 Thanks @taiiiyang! - feat(subtitles): request AI subtitles on X (Twitter) videos. A post with several videos transcribes the one being played, live broadcasts are turned away, a transcript of another video shows its own message instead of the wrong subtitles, and moving the pointer over another video while AI subtitles load no longer drops them

  • #2569 a5d61fb Thanks @taiiiyang! - fix(subtitles): show the Read Frog subtitles button on videos in X's media view, the full-screen viewer that opens over a post, so their captions can be translated and AI subtitles requested there too

anomalyco/opencode ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.4.1 Stable2026-11-23T15:57:40Z

Highlights

  • macOS 12 Session Fix: OpenCode can save assistant messages again on the older SQLite version included with macOS 12. (#65771)
  • Config-Only Providers Show Their Models: Providers you set up only in your config, such as Ollama, now show their models in /models without a /connect login. (#65793)
  • Unread /btw Answers: When a side question finishes behind another side-panel tab, its tab shows an unread dot until you open it. (#65821)

Core

Improvements

  • With experimental.model_speeds enabled, model service tiers are exposed as remembered speeds instead of separate model-picker entries. (#65749)

Bug fixes

  • Fixed assistant messages failing to save on macOS 12 because its system SQLite does not support the JSON operator used in v2.4.0. (#65771)
  • Fixed compaction retaining more recent history than keep.tokens, including when a single newest message already exceeds the allowance. (#65768)
  • Fixed occasional extra cache cost in long goal sessions after a compaction. (#65788)

TUI

Improvements

  • With experimental.model_speeds enabled, /speed and /fast list the current model’s service tiers and relative prices. The speed.list and speed.cycle keybinds are available without default bindings. (#65749)

Bug fixes

  • Fixed /models hiding models from config-only providers such as Ollama when no /connect login exists. (#65793)

Desktop

Improvements

  • With experimental.model_speeds enabled, models with service tiers have a speed dropdown beside the variant selector; /speed cycles through the available speeds. (#65749)
  • Layout choices in Settings now use consistent navigation-card previews and accent styling. (#65757, #65800)

Bug fixes

  • Fixed /btw answers completed behind another side-panel tab providing no unread indication. The tab now shows a dot until viewed. (#65821)
  • Fixed the timeline, Home list, file viewers and tool output adding invisible or oversized stops to keyboard Tab navigation. They remain focusable by clicking for keyboard scrolling. (#65758, #65785)
  • Fixed unwanted focus rings around clicked Settings pages, error details, What’s New and the terminal, and corrected the split button’s focus-ring corners. (#65783, #65785)
  • Fixed folder paths showing middle-truncation markers unnecessarily at fractional UI zoom levels. (#65225)

SDK

Bug fixes

  • An aborted shared event subscription now stops immediately even when the source has already completed or failed with buffered events remaining. Thanks @chirag127. (#65441)

Extensions

Improvements

  • Effect and Promise plugins can create, list and delete automations through ctx.experimental.automation.{create,list,delete}. (#65747)
  • @opencode/plugin now supports @opentui/core and @opentui/solid from version 0.8.0, allowing plugins capped below OpenTUI 0.9 to install again. (#65819)

📦 下载说明

主程序（MCP 服务）：

  • macOS Apple Silicon: xiaohongshu-mcp-darwin-arm64
  • Windows x64: xiaohongshu-mcp-windows-amd64.exe
  • Linux x64: xiaohongshu-mcp-linux-amd64

登录工具：

  • macOS Apple Silicon: xiaohongshu-login-darwin-arm64
  • Windows x64: xiaohongshu-login-windows-amd64.exe
  • Linux x64: xiaohongshu-login-linux-amd64

🔧 使用方法

# 1. 首先运行登录工具获取 cookie
./xiaohongshu-login-darwin-arm64

# 2. 然后启动 MCP 服务
./xiaohongshu-mcp-darwin-arm64

# 或指定参数
./xiaohongshu-mcp-darwin-arm64 -headless=false

⚠️ 注意事项

  • 首次运行时会自动下载无头浏览器（约 150MB），请确保网络连接正常
  • 后续运行无需重复下载浏览器
  • 登录工具生成的 cookie 保存在 ~/.xiaohongshu/cookies.json
